<script type="text/javascript">
		var ajaxurl = 'https://www.singletracks.com/blog/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php';
		jQuery(function() {
			var newsoffset = 6;
			var topicoffset = 5;
			jQuery('.ra_featured_card').click( function(){
				location.href = jQuery( this ).data('url');
			});
			jQuery('#st_load_more_news_button').click( function(){
				ra_featured_story_load_latest( newsoffset );
				newsoffset += 6;
			});
			jQuery('#st_load_more_topics_button').click( function(){
				ra_featured_story_load_topics( topicoffset );
				topicoffset += 5;
			});

			
			'use strict';



		});	
		
		function ra_featured_story_load_latest( offset ){
			jQuery.getJSON(ajaxurl+'?action=ra_featured_story_latest&offset='+offset, function( data ){
				jQuery('#st_latest_news').append( data.html );
				// if ( offset == 6 ){scrollToAnchor( 'news_end' );}
			});
		}
		function ra_featured_story_load_topics( offset ){
			jQuery.getJSON(ajaxurl+'?action=ra_featured_story_topics&offset='+offset, function( data ){
				jQuery('#st_latest_forums').append( data.html );
				// if ( offset == 5 ){scrollToAnchor( 'topics_end' );}
			});
		}
		function scrollToAnchor(aid){
			var aTag = jQuery("a[name='"+ aid +"']");
			jQuery('html,body').animate({scrollTop: aTag.offset().top-150},'slow');
		}	
		function st_select_episode(id){
			jQuery('.st_episode_player').hide();
			jQuery('#st_player_'+id).toggle();
			jQuery('#st_podcast_cover').css('background-image', 'url(' + st_cover_art[id] + ')' );
		}	
	</script>

<div class="nf_byline">
									by Matt Miller
								</div>
<div class="nf_preview_text">
